        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Sloop, traversing large sized bodies of water should be relatively easy. To make sure you dont end up at the mercy of the wind you can lower your Anchor to stop in place.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye your sails with dye of your choice!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Sloop.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Try not to sail through rivers with a sloop, it will likely NOT work as expected!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Where's the Wind Waker when you need it?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Sloop",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Rowboat, you can now transport a decent amount of items across the oceans, perfect for overseas exploration, perhaps to the tropics! Make sure you create two Oars so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Rowboat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tips: Two passenger slots? Perfect for bringing home pairs of breeding animals for your farm through a river system or across lakes and oceans.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Rowboat-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:rowboats",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Kayak in hand, you can now enjoy your quick solo journeys on water. Make sure you craft a Kayak Paddle (the canoe one won't work!) to go even faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Kayak-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Canoe, traversing bodies of water in the early game will now be possible. Make sure you create a Canoe Paddle so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at the finished Canoe.",

        },
        {

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Canoe",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak is the second most simple boat you can craft, requiring Waxes for the Waterproof Hides. It is also the only boat that can be picked up as an item. Despite having a single passenger slot, it is the fastest boat in the game until the steel age Sloop!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Requiring more complex materials, locking it to the bronze age, the Rowboat has capacity for 2 passengers and 2 storage, or 4 storage with no passengers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need to find some sort of Hardwood, detailed in the Field Guide, which also includes the construction process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind the boat must be constructed with the same wood! Do not mix different kinds of hardwoods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Sloop is the most advanced seaworthy vessel, requiring Wrought Iron. It has ample space for storage, anvils and workbenches! It is incredibly expensive and requires practice to sail. The Field Guide has all the details for construction and on how to sail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Again, do not attempt to mix up different kinds of hardwoods in the same vessel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "tips__transportation",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Sloop, traversing large sized bodies of water should be relatively easy. To make sure you dont end up at the mercy of the wind you can lower your Anchor to stop in place.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye your sails with dye of your choice!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Sloop.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Try not to sail through rivers with a sloop, it will likely NOT work as expected!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Where's the Wind Waker when you need it?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Sloop",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Rowboat, you can now transport a decent amount of items across the oceans, perfect for overseas exploration, perhaps to the tropics! Make sure you create two Oars so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Rowboat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tips: Two passenger slots? Perfect for bringing home pairs of breeding animals for your farm through a river system or across lakes and oceans.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Rowboat-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:rowboats",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Kayak in hand, you can now enjoy your quick solo journeys on water. Make sure you craft a Kayak Paddle (the canoe one won't work!) to go even faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Kayak-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Canoe, traversing bodies of water in the early game will now be possible. Make sure you create a Canoe Paddle so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at the finished Canoe.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Canoe",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak is the second most simple boat you can craft, requiring Waxes for the Waterproof Hides. It is also the only boat that can be picked up as an item. Despite having a single passenger slot, it is the fastest boat in the game until the steel age Sloop!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",

          "key": "quest-snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {

        {

        {

        {

          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Requiring more complex materials, locking it to the bronze age, the Rowboat has capacity for 2 passengers and 2 storage, or 4 storage with no passengers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need to find some sort of Hardwood, detailed in the Field Guide, which also includes the construction process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind the boat must be constructed with the same wood! Do not mix different kinds of hardwoods.",

        {

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Sloop is the most advanced seaworthy vessel, requiring Wrought Iron. It has ample space for storage, anvils and workbenches! It is incredibly expensive and requires practice to sail. The Field Guide has all the details for construction and on how to sail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Again, do not attempt to mix up different kinds of hardwoods in the same vessel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

    {
      "id": "create:artificial/intelligence",
      "label": "Artificial Intelligence",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 1,
      "evidence": [
        {

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:deployer",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/deployer.json",
          "text": "Artificial Intelligence",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Artificial Intelligence",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:deployer",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/deployer.json",
          "text": "Place and power a Deployer, the perfect reflection of yourself",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Artificial Intelligence",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:fist_bump",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/fist_bump.json",
          "text": "Pound It, Bro!",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Pound It, Bro!",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:fist_bump",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/fist_bump.json",
          "text": "Make two Deployers fist-bump (Hidden Advancement)",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Pound It, Bro!",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "questssteam_age",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Millstone is an automatic version of the Quern. You can throw whatever you'd like into the top, and then right-click to take your crushed items back out. It's pretty slow if you connect it directly to your Animal Crank, but you can use gear ratios to increase its speed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You will only receive the output in the first slot. The other slots are part of a GregTech mechanic that won't be relevant until much later (HV).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The millstone can't be used to process grains into flour because Create doesn't understand TFC's food expiry system, which previously led to all sorts of bugs involving rotten items becoming fresh and vice versa. Until you're able to get the Food Processor in LV, you can still crush your grains via a Mortar in a crafting grid.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "No more querning",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Automatic Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Animal Crank is your first accessible source of mechanical power. To use it, place the crank on the center of a 7x7 cleared area and leash an animal to it. Different animals will provide different amounts of power, while the blocks underneath will increase the speed of the output power. The area of multiple cranks can overlap.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You may need to hold a second lead to attach an animal.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Small Animals (16 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Wolf, Dog, Pig, Sheep, Goat, Alpaca",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Medium Animals (24 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Cow, Donkey",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Large Animals (32 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Horse, Mule, Musk Ox, Yak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Make your livestock pay rent",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Your First Power Source",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Does crafting all those plates for a Bloomery look tedious? The Helve Hammer will automatically (but slowly) flatten double ingots into plates for you. You'll still have to do the welding yourself though. Simply place an anvil directly under the hammer and throw your double ingots onto it by pressing the Throw Item key (Default: Q), then right-click the hammer to get your plates out.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Higher tier anvils will also require fewer hits to make plates from the previous tier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to use cogs to increase its speed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "It may be unimpressive to start, but once you're able to increase its speed to 256 RPM with Steel cogs and shafts, the Helve Hammer will really start to shine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "CLANG... CLANG... CLANG...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Helve Hammer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate is a fusion of Create and GregTech that has different tiered versions of Create's machines. It also introduces stress limits on Shafts and Cogwheels, so you'll have to plan more about how you power your machinery!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Generally speaking, you'll want smaller setups with their own individual power sources, instead of doing the usual Create thing of having a blob of water wheels connected into a single Shaft into your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of Create? This'll make it more interesting",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Basics of Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels provide much more power than the Animal Crank, at the expense of requiring flowing water. You don't have the ability to move water sources yet, so you'll need to find a river to power these. Oceans and Lakes can also provide a little power, but if you're far inland, you're better off looking at building a windmill.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You can get treated wood by soaking any planks in creosote from your Coke Oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Hope you set up your base near a river!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "If you aren't near flowing water, a windmill is another effective option for a better source of power. Bigger windmills provide more stress units and faster speeds.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Pairs well with tulips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "These are the basis of most of the simple machines you can build with Create. Check JEI for all the different things you can craft with this, including Gearboxes and other ways to control your stress units.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "New to Create? There's many tutorials online about different kinds of contraptions you can build, and most of them will still work in TFG too! (Except anything involving cobble generators)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Primitive Machine Hull?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Metal Casings",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Encased Fan is the final step in your first fully automatic ore processing line, so you no longer need to clean your impure dusts by hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate's Encased Fans work a little differently than in base Create. Instead of placing a water source in front of the fan, you'll need to pump water into the encased fan block itself. Be sure to set the number on the fan to 1!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Next, place your crushed ore or dusts on a Depot or Belt in front of the fan (the floor will NOT work!), and they'll be gleaming in no time. As a bonus, it'll give you some extra byproducts too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Great for hot days",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Encased Fan",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Age, now you can automate fluids as well as items! The Mechanical Pump can pull fluids out of pretty much anything, including barrels, crucibles, and even source blocks. You can use it with either Create's or GregTech's fluid pipes, though each behaves a little differently.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Create's pipes have no internal fluid capacity, instead transporting fluids directly from source to destination. You can right-click them with a Copper Casing to \"lock\" any connections in place, and right-clicking them with a wrench will open a little window that prevents intersections, so you can run multiple pipes next to each other. Create's pipes also require a mechanical pump to use, and each pump only has a range of 16 blocks, but one pump can pull fluids from multiple sources at once.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "They also have no temperature limit!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "On the other hand, GregTech's pipes do have their own internal fluid capacity and don't require a pump once fluid is inside them. Like item pipes, their connections can also be fully controlled by sneak right-clicking them with an empty off-hand, and we recommend doing so on all your pipes otherwise the fluid sloshes around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Are your mechanical pumps and GregTech pipes not connecting? Flip the pump twice (right-click with a wrench) and that should get them working together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Delicious pipe spaghetti",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid Handling",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines need to be powered! You have three options to generate steam: the Solid boiler accepts solid fuels like coal and charcoal, the Liquid boiler accepts liquids like creosote and lava, and the Solar boiler requires no fuel but only works during the day. The choice is yours, but we recommend the liquid one if you're unsure.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines all have a vent which needs to stay unblocked in order for them to work properly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Boilers will explode if you attempt to put water into them while they are dry and hot. It's best to always have water being pumped into them, but you can always break and place the block to reset its temperature.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Portable Steamroom",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hp_steam_solid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_liquid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_solar_boiler))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Any High Pressure Steam Boiler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Boilers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels and windmills not cutting it any more? It wouldn't be a steam age without a proper steam engine! Each steam engine can output a whole 256 SU, giving you much more power to play with.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Blaze Burners can be crafted without leaving your house, and you can use a Mechanical Arm to automatically feed them solid fuel, or directly pipe liquid fuel (such as creosote). Coal Gems, Anthracite, and Coke can also be used to superheat them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some real steam power",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Engine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:brass",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/brass.json",
          "text": "Create Brass Ingots by alloying Copper and Zinc Ingots in your Blaze-heated Mechanical Mixer",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Real Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Primitive Alloys",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Alloys are a method of mixing two or more metals together, to create a new, stronger metal. During the early game, while copper is a useful metal for creating tools, the next tier of metal is one of three types of Bronze. An alloy is made up of component metals which must each satisfy a specific percentage of the overall whole.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "One method through which alloys can be made during the early game is through the usage of a Small Vessel. The process is very similar to Casting. However, instead of using just a single metal, place enough ore pieces inside the vessel in the correct ratio to form a known alloy mix.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "For example, to create 1000 mB of Bronze (shown to the right), you would need between 700 and 800 mB of Copper, and between 200 and 300 mB of Tin. The next three pages show the recipes of the three bronzes. Each type of bronze can be used to make tools, Armor, and other metal items. They are slightly different so resulting tools will have different durability, efficiency, and attack damage.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 70 - 80 % : Copper - 20 - 30 % : Tin",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Bismuth Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 20 - 30 % : Zinc - 50 - 65 % : Copper - 10 - 20 % : Bismuth",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Black Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Copper - 10 - 25 % : Silver - 10 - 25 % : Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Other alloys are not suitable for creating tools. These are Brass, Rose Gold, and Sterling Silver. Brass is a useful alloy for all sorts of contraptions, including Mechanical Power. Rose Gold and Sterling Silver are used for crafting Red Steel and Blue Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
