        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Go and start working on those Low Voltage components!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Basic mechanical power starts here",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:bronze_anvils)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:bronze_anvils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T2 Anvil: Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "create:deployer",
      "label": "Deployer",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 1,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:recipes/misc/crafting/kinetics/deployer",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "questssteam_age",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Millstone is an automatic version of the Quern. You can throw whatever you'd like into the top, and then right-click to take your crushed items back out. It's pretty slow if you connect it directly to your Animal Crank, but you can use gear ratios to increase its speed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You will only receive the output in the first slot. The other slots are part of a GregTech mechanic that won't be relevant until much later (HV).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The millstone can't be used to process grains into flour because Create doesn't understand TFC's food expiry system, which previously led to all sorts of bugs involving rotten items becoming fresh and vice versa. Until you're able to get the Food Processor in LV, you can still crush your grains via a Mortar in a crafting grid.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "No more querning",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Automatic Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Animal Crank is your first accessible source of mechanical power. To use it, place the crank on the center of a 7x7 cleared area and leash an animal to it. Different animals will provide different amounts of power, while the blocks underneath will increase the speed of the output power. The area of multiple cranks can overlap.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You may need to hold a second lead to attach an animal.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Small Animals (16 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Wolf, Dog, Pig, Sheep, Goat, Alpaca",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Medium Animals (24 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Cow, Donkey",

        },
        {

        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Horse, Mule, Musk Ox, Yak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Make your livestock pay rent",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Your First Power Source",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Does crafting all those plates for a Bloomery look tedious? The Helve Hammer will automatically (but slowly) flatten double ingots into plates for you. You'll still have to do the welding yourself though. Simply place an anvil directly under the hammer and throw your double ingots onto it by pressing the Throw Item key (Default: Q), then right-click the hammer to get your plates out.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Higher tier anvils will also require fewer hits to make plates from the previous tier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to use cogs to increase its speed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "It may be unimpressive to start, but once you're able to increase its speed to 256 RPM with Steel cogs and shafts, the Helve Hammer will really start to shine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "CLANG... CLANG... CLANG...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Helve Hammer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate is a fusion of Create and GregTech that has different tiered versions of Create's machines. It also introduces stress limits on Shafts and Cogwheels, so you'll have to plan more about how you power your machinery!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Generally speaking, you'll want smaller setups with their own individual power sources, instead of doing the usual Create thing of having a blob of water wheels connected into a single Shaft into your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of Create? This'll make it more interesting",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Basics of Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels provide much more power than the Animal Crank, at the expense of requiring flowing water. You don't have the ability to move water sources yet, so you'll need to find a river to power these. Oceans and Lakes can also provide a little power, but if you're far inland, you're better off looking at building a windmill.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You can get treated wood by soaking any planks in creosote from your Coke Oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Hope you set up your base near a river!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "If you aren't near flowing water, a windmill is another effective option for a better source of power. Bigger windmills provide more stress units and faster speeds.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Pairs well with tulips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "These are the basis of most of the simple machines you can build with Create. Check JEI for all the different things you can craft with this, including Gearboxes and other ways to control your stress units.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "New to Create? There's many tutorials online about different kinds of contraptions you can build, and most of them will still work in TFG too! (Except anything involving cobble generators)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Primitive Machine Hull?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Metal Casings",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Encased Fan is the final step in your first fully automatic ore processing line, so you no longer need to clean your impure dusts by hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate's Encased Fans work a little differently than in base Create. Instead of placing a water source in front of the fan, you'll need to pump water into the encased fan block itself. Be sure to set the number on the fan to 1!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Next, place your crushed ore or dusts on a Depot or Belt in front of the fan (the floor will NOT work!), and they'll be gleaming in no time. As a bonus, it'll give you some extra byproducts too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Great for hot days",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Encased Fan",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Age, now you can automate fluids as well as items! The Mechanical Pump can pull fluids out of pretty much anything, including barrels, crucibles, and even source blocks. You can use it with either Create's or GregTech's fluid pipes, though each behaves a little differently.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Create's pipes have no internal fluid capacity, instead transporting fluids directly from source to destination. You can right-click them with a Copper Casing to \"lock\" any connections in place, and right-clicking them with a wrench will open a little window that prevents intersections, so you can run multiple pipes next to each other. Create's pipes also require a mechanical pump to use, and each pump only has a range of 16 blocks, but one pump can pull fluids from multiple sources at once.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "They also have no temperature limit!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "On the other hand, GregTech's pipes do have their own internal fluid capacity and don't require a pump once fluid is inside them. Like item pipes, their connections can also be fully controlled by sneak right-clicking them with an empty off-hand, and we recommend doing so on all your pipes otherwise the fluid sloshes around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Are your mechanical pumps and GregTech pipes not connecting? Flip the pump twice (right-click with a wrench) and that should get them working together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Delicious pipe spaghetti",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid Handling",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines need to be powered! You have three options to generate steam: the Solid boiler accepts solid fuels like coal and charcoal, the Liquid boiler accepts liquids like creosote and lava, and the Solar boiler requires no fuel but only works during the day. The choice is yours, but we recommend the liquid one if you're unsure.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines all have a vent which needs to stay unblocked in order for them to work properly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Boilers will explode if you attempt to put water into them while they are dry and hot. It's best to always have water being pumped into them, but you can always break and place the block to reset its temperature.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Portable Steamroom",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hp_steam_solid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_liquid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_solar_boiler))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Any High Pressure Steam Boiler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Boilers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels and windmills not cutting it any more? It wouldn't be a steam age without a proper steam engine! Each steam engine can output a whole 256 SU, giving you much more power to play with.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Blaze Burners can be crafted without leaving your house, and you can use a Mechanical Arm to automatically feed them solid fuel, or directly pipe liquid fuel (such as creosote). Coal Gems, Anthracite, and Coke can also be used to superheat them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some real steam power",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Engine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of chopping trees? These will do it for you! Place them on a spinning contraption and watch them go. Unfortunately, you'll still have to replant the saplings them yourself until you get access to a Deployer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "A Mechanical Saw can also double as a stonecutter, giving easier access to various forms of masonry. Using it to chop logs into lumber is also more efficient!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You can also use it to cut Rods into four Bolts!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Efficient cutting of wood, stone, and your fingers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "create:mechanical/press",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:andesite_casing",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/andesite_casing.json",
          "text": "The Andesite Age",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "The Andesite Age",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "create:andesite_casing",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!data/create/advancements/andesite_casing.json",
          "text": "Apply Andesite Alloy to stripped wood, creating a basic casing for your machines",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "The Andesite Age",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "questssteam_age",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Millstone is an automatic version of the Quern. You can throw whatever you'd like into the top, and then right-click to take your crushed items back out. It's pretty slow if you connect it directly to your Animal Crank, but you can use gear ratios to increase its speed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You will only receive the output in the first slot. The other slots are part of a GregTech mechanic that won't be relevant until much later (HV).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Lore: The millstone can't be used to process grains into flour because Create doesn't understand TFC's food expiry system, which previously led to all sorts of bugs involving rotten items becoming fresh and vice versa. Until you're able to get the Food Processor in LV, you can still crush your grains via a Mortar in a crafting grid.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "No more querning",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Automatic Ore Processing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Animal Crank is your first accessible source of mechanical power. To use it, place the crank on the center of a 7x7 cleared area and leash an animal to it. Different animals will provide different amounts of power, while the blocks underneath will increase the speed of the output power. The area of multiple cranks can overlap.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You may need to hold a second lead to attach an animal.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Small Animals (16 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Wolf, Dog, Pig, Sheep, Goat, Alpaca",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Medium Animals (24 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Cow, Donkey",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Large Animals (32 SU):",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Horse, Mule, Musk Ox, Yak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Make your livestock pay rent",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Your First Power Source",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Does crafting all those plates for a Bloomery look tedious? The Helve Hammer will automatically (but slowly) flatten double ingots into plates for you. You'll still have to do the welding yourself though. Simply place an anvil directly under the hammer and throw your double ingots onto it by pressing the Throw Item key (Default: Q), then right-click the hammer to get your plates out.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Higher tier anvils will also require fewer hits to make plates from the previous tier.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to use cogs to increase its speed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "It may be unimpressive to start, but once you're able to increase its speed to 256 RPM with Steel cogs and shafts, the Helve Hammer will really start to shine.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "CLANG... CLANG... CLANG...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Helve Hammer",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate is a fusion of Create and GregTech that has different tiered versions of Create's machines. It also introduces stress limits on Shafts and Cogwheels, so you'll have to plan more about how you power your machinery!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Generally speaking, you'll want smaller setups with their own individual power sources, instead of doing the usual Create thing of having a blob of water wheels connected into a single Shaft into your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of Create? This'll make it more interesting",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Basics of Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels provide much more power than the Animal Crank, at the expense of requiring flowing water. You don't have the ability to move water sources yet, so you'll need to find a river to power these. Oceans and Lakes can also provide a little power, but if you're far inland, you're better off looking at building a windmill.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "You can get treated wood by soaking any planks in creosote from your Coke Oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Hope you set up your base near a river!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water Wheels",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "If you aren't near flowing water, a windmill is another effective option for a better source of power. Bigger windmills provide more stress units and faster speeds.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Pairs well with tulips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "These are the basis of most of the simple machines you can build with Create. Check JEI for all the different things you can craft with this, including Gearboxes and other ways to control your stress units.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "New to Create? There's many tutorials online about different kinds of contraptions you can build, and most of them will still work in TFG too! (Except anything involving cobble generators)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Primitive Machine Hull?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Metal Casings",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "The Encased Fan is the final step in your first fully automatic ore processing line, so you no longer need to clean your impure dusts by hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Greate's Encased Fans work a little differently than in base Create. Instead of placing a water source in front of the fan, you'll need to pump water into the encased fan block itself. Be sure to set the number on the fan to 1!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Next, place your crushed ore or dusts on a Depot or Belt in front of the fan (the floor will NOT work!), and they'll be gleaming in no time. As a bonus, it'll give you some extra byproducts too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Great for hot days",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Encased Fan",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Age, now you can automate fluids as well as items! The Mechanical Pump can pull fluids out of pretty much anything, including barrels, crucibles, and even source blocks. You can use it with either Create's or GregTech's fluid pipes, though each behaves a little differently.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Create's pipes have no internal fluid capacity, instead transporting fluids directly from source to destination. You can right-click them with a Copper Casing to \"lock\" any connections in place, and right-clicking them with a wrench will open a little window that prevents intersections, so you can run multiple pipes next to each other. Create's pipes also require a mechanical pump to use, and each pump only has a range of 16 blocks, but one pump can pull fluids from multiple sources at once.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "They also have no temperature limit!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "On the other hand, GregTech's pipes do have their own internal fluid capacity and don't require a pump once fluid is inside them. Like item pipes, their connections can also be fully controlled by sneak right-clicking them with an empty off-hand, and we recommend doing so on all your pipes otherwise the fluid sloshes around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Are your mechanical pumps and GregTech pipes not connecting? Flip the pump twice (right-click with a wrench) and that should get them working together.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Delicious pipe spaghetti",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Fluid Handling",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines need to be powered! You have three options to generate steam: the Solid boiler accepts solid fuels like coal and charcoal, the Liquid boiler accepts liquids like creosote and lava, and the Solar boiler requires no fuel but only works during the day. The choice is yours, but we recommend the liquid one if you're unsure.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam machines all have a vent which needs to stay unblocked in order for them to work properly.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Warning: Boilers will explode if you attempt to put water into them while they are dry and hot. It's best to always have water being pumped into them, but you can always break and place the block to reset its temperature.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Portable Steamroom",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:hp_steam_solid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_liquid_boiler)item(gtceu:hp_steam_solar_boiler))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Any High Pressure Steam Boiler",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Boilers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Water wheels and windmills not cutting it any more? It wouldn't be a steam age without a proper steam engine! Each steam engine can output a whole 256 SU, giving you much more power to play with.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Blaze Burners can be crafted without leaving your house, and you can use a Mechanical Arm to automatically feed them solid fuel, or directly pipe liquid fuel (such as creosote). Coal Gems, Anthracite, and Coke can also be used to superheat them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Time for some real steam power",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Steam Engine",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "Tired of chopping trees? These will do it for you! Place them on a spinning contraption and watch them go. Unfortunately, you'll still have to replant the saplings them yourself until you get access to a Deployer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questssteam_age",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questssteam_age.snbt",
          "text": "A Mechanical Saw can also double as a stonecutter, giving easier access to various forms of masonry. Using it to chop logs into lumber is also more efficient!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "questssteam_age",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "firmalife:hydrophobic",

      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
