          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you make too much noise in the desert, you might attract the attention of the mighty Sandworm! It's more of an environmental hazard than a boss to kill, so your best way of surviving it is to run away!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you manage to hit its head enough times with explosion damage, it will leave you alone... for a while.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Thankfully, the Sandworm can only damage entities, not buildings, and is only attracted to the footsteps of players, so any way to keep your own feet off the ground won't anger it. The atmosphere is too thin for airplanes and hang gliders, but there are other tools...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "SHAI-HULUD",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Sandworm",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum Deposits are immovable blocks that you can only find in the Martian Deep Desert, far away from any of the more lush areas. They spawn in small blobs, and can be broken to make space for your Ostrum Harvester if they're on a slope. Try using a Spyglass to find them from a distance!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You'll have to figure out some long-distance logistics to ship your Ostrum back to your base. (Try a train!)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum is a crucial resource for both EV progression and making infinite ores from Mars.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Spice Melange",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The spice must flow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum Deposits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is Mars not cold enough for you?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Head up (or down) towards one of its poles at Z=+15000/-5000. If anything's able to live there, surely it must have amazing insulative properties...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Cold enough for CO₂ to freeze!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Poles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Heavy Ammoniacal Water is another kind of fluid that can only be found deep underground via a Fluid Drilling Rig. You can only find it in these lush areas of Mars, not the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can separate it into Heavy Water, which is important for Fission power.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Cooler Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Heavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Unlike regular Sheep on Earth, killing a Glacian one will only give you normal Wool. You'll need to build up their familiarity and shear them if you want the special Glacian Wool.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need 7 of it for a full Tungstensteel Space Suit, but you can also use it as a great component for your Fission Reactor to increase its maximum heat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also turn it into regular Wool Yarn and Cloth if you want more of that for some reason.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "What an incredible insulator!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Glacian Wool",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Airplanes don't work on Mars, and your space suit doesn't have a jetpack, so there's no other way to fly, right?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you're able to find an elusive Soarer, you can tame them with Raw Fish and equip them with a Saddle to glide through the Martian skies with ease.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "They can't fly forever though, indicated by the wing icons on the top left, and will need to land occasionally. (You can feed them more Raw Fish to speed up their recovery.)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Still, they're a great option for long-distance travel, and won't upset the sandworm either!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Note: Soarers are unable to survive on particularly hot planets such as Venus.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sniffers and Wraptors can be found all over Mars and can be domesticated for meat, eggs, and most importantly, their mineral-rich tufts and feathers.",

          "text": "Hydrogen is an essential component of countless MV+ chemical processes, such as acids or ammonia.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts by electrolyzing water or by reacting water with the Formic Acid loop.",

          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Deuterium and Tritium are used in LuV for fusion and can be obtained by centrifuging hydrogen or electrolyzing heavy water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium is used in HV+ to cool down hot ingots in the vacuum freezer and to speed up the EBF.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in HV from Liquid Earth Air distillation and with the Fluid Drilling Rig on the moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium-3 is used for recipes on the Moon and can be obtained by a Fluid Drilling Rig on the Moon or by centrifuging helium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Lithium is the best electrolyte for LV, MV, and HV batteries.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It is abundant in Tungstate and Scheelite veins. The tungsten line yields lithium as a byproduct.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Beryllium is widely used for nuclear fission, including fissile fuel rods, Heat Batteries, and fuels such as FLiBe. It is also used in HV to create Ender Dust.",

          "label": "chemical_elements",

        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Boron is a component of borosilicate glass used in MV+ to make gem lenses and in HV+ to make AE2 cables, Vitrified Ender Pearls, and Fiber-Reinforced Circuit Boards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",

          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Carbon is the base of organic chemistry. It is used in LV+ to make resistors and in HV+ to make carbon fibers.",

          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Fluorine is used in HV+ for polytetrafluoroethylene.",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in MV from electrolyzing various ores such as Topaz. Unlike other gaseous elements, fluorine cannot be obtained in infinite amounts, but Topaz and Bastnatite is fluorine-rich and abundant on the Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",

        {

        {

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Space is home to many other kinds of animals than those you'd find on Earth. A few animals are hostile and will attack the player, but most are passive. Only a few of them are domesticatable. Animals you find on other worlds can be divided into two categories: hot and cold. Animals can only survive on planets that either have a similar climate to where they came from, or on temperate planets like Earth.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Space animals do not require an oxygenated environment, but it will make the area count as \"temperate\" for their survival. For example, if you bring a cold-dwelling animal (like a Sniffer) to a hot planet (like Venus), it will catch on fire, unless it's inside an oxygenated environment. This makes Earth the ideal place to build a zoo, if you'd like. You can transport animals between planets using AE2's Spatial Storage system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Moon Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The Moon may be pretty barren, but it still has a few animals that call it home. It's considered a Cold world despite the pretty extreme temperature swings.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Moon Rabbit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Besides their coloring, Moon Rabbits have identical stats to Earth rabbits. They also eat Chorus Fruit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Limpets are a type of snail with ore on their shell. You can obtain the ore with any mining tool. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Star Crawler",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Star Crawlers are a type of starfish. They are hostile and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Birts are a type of bird? They lay eggs in their nests, are passive unless attacked, and can be eaten. Their eggs can be thrown to stun targets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Mars Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Mars is the planet with an environment closest to Earth's, featuring a wide variety of animals to interact with and crops to feed them (and yourself) with... outside of the huge desert, where almost nothing lives. Mars is a Cold planet.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The Sandworm",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Sandworms are alerted by player footsteps. They are the reason almost nothing else lives in the desert.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Sniffers are domesticatable animals found between -30 and -102°C. They require a Large Nest to lay their eggs and can be shorn for Sniffer Tufts. Their eggs can be cooked or extracted for Cream. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A sniffer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Wraptors are domesticatable animals found between -15 and -100°C. They require a Large Nest to lay their eggs and can be shorn for Wraptor Feathers. Their eggs can be cooked or extracted for Wraptor Sugar. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A wraptor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Glacian Sheep",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Preferring extremely cold environments below -108°C, Glacian Sheep are domesticatable animals. They can be shorn for Glacian Wool, an amazing insulator. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A glacian ram/ewe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Snatchers are passive animals that like to steal your stuff! Attack them to retrieve your items, or feed them Raw Fish to tame them. Once tamed, you can carry them on your back and equip them with a wooden chest. Press to throw them, and they will steal the contents of a nearby chest and bring it to you.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A snatcher.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Chargers are semipassive animals. You can tame them with any Mars crop to use them as a combat land mount. Press for a headbutt attack, or press and hold Spacebar for a charge attack. They can also be equipped with dyeable armor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Chargers have a natural rivalry with Raiders. They will attack each other on sight.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Surfers are semiaquatic passive animals. You can tame them with Raw Fish to ride them underwater. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Soarers are passive animals found above -106°C. You can tame them with Raw Fish to use them as a flying mount. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A top predator, Eaters are hostile animals found above -109°C. If you can manage to take one down, they can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Crunchers are territorial miniboss predators. Feed them meat to stop their regeneration. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Despite their name, Gliders cannot be tamed or ridden. They are passive and live in areas above -105°C. They enjoy music and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Crushers are semihostile animals found in herds in areas above -100°C. They can be killed for their meat and hard spikes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Springlings are wild animals found above -108°C. Feeding them Saplings will let you ride them and extend their extremely long necks. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Goobers are lazy wild animals found above -109°C. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The gigantic Walkers inhabit areas above -97°C. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Raiders are hostile animals found above -109°C. They have a natural rivalry with Chargers. You can make them neutral by feeding them Raw Meat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Toxlacanths are aquatic prey animals. It is recommended to kill them from a distance and cook their meat before eating.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "One of the few animals that can survive the Martian desert, Stackaticks are passive prey animals. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.31.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {

    {
      "id": "tfc:dropping/the/anvil",
      "label": "Dropping the Anvil",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 1,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/metal_anvil",
          "confidence": 0.65
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/metal_anvil",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/metal_anvil.json",
          "text": "Dropping the Anvil",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Dropping the Anvil",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/metal_anvil",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/metal_anvil.json",
          "text": "Create a copper anvil out of double ingots",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Dropping the Anvil",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Anvils are an important tool required for metalworking, as they allow you to work and weld metal ingots into various different forms. Working is done to form one piece of metal into another, and welding, is used to fuse two metal items into one solid piece.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "All types of metal anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Anvils can be crafted with double ingots of their respective metal. For your first anvil, you must first weld double ingots on a stone anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Anvils each have a tier, which defines what types of material they can work and weld. An anvil can work metals of its current tier, and it can weld metals that are one tier higher. - Tier 0: Stone Anvils - Tier I: Copper - Tier II: Bismuth Bronze, Black Bronze, Bronze - Tier III: Wrought Iron - Tier IV: Steel - Tier V: Black Steel - Tier VI: Red Steel, Blue Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "In order to work an item on the anvil, you will need to use the anvil, to open up the anvil interface, seen to the right. On the left, there are two input slots for items - for working, the target item must be in the right hand slot. You will also need a hammer while working, either in the hammer slot on the right of the anvil or in your main hand. The hammer will gradually take damage as you work the item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "The anvil interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "You will then need to select the plan, which chooses which item you want to create. on the the scroll button, and then pick one of the items to create. The anvil interface will return, but now you will have selected a plan - the scroll will show the item you are working to create, and the rules and target will now be populated.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "After selecting to create a pickaxe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "In the middle of the anvil screen, there is a bar with two colored indicators. The green pointer, is your current working progress. The red pointer, is the target. Your goal is to line up the current progress, with the target. In order to do this, you can use the green and red action buttons, which move your current progress a certain amount, depending on the action taken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Green actions will always move your target right, and Red actions will always move your progress left. Note that if you move your target off of the progress bar, you will have overworked your item - you will lose the ingot. However, while working, you must also match the rules...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "The rules, are the two or three icons shown on the top of the anvil interface. They represent specific actions that must be taken, at specific times, in order for your working to be a success. For example, a rule could be Bend Second Last, meaning the second to last action you take must be a Bend action.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Working Rules",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Your last three actions are shown right underneath the rules. When a rule is satisfied, its outline will change to green. Success occurs when all rules are satisfied. Finally, you have to be mindful of your item's temperature. Metals can only be worked when they are above a certain temperature where the tooltip shows \"Can Work\". You may take an item out and re-heat it during the working process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Working can be tedious, and take many steps to get correct. However, there is a reward for being efficient. Some items, such as tool heads, when they are worked in a low or minimal amount of steps, receive a Forging Bonus based on how efficiently they were forged. This bonus will then apply to tools that the item is used in, for example, a pickaxe head used to make a pickaxe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Forging Bonuses",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Perfectly Forged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "There are four tiers of forging bonus: - Poorly Forged - Well Forged - Expertly Forged - Perfectly Forged These bonuses increase the power of your tool - making it break less often, mine faster, and/or do more damage in combat, depending on the tool.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Welding is a process through which two items are fused together to create a new item. Welding works the same whether on a Stone Anvil or a metal anvil. First, you must place the two items you want to weld on the anvil. You can do this either by using the items on the anvil, or by opening the anvil interface and inserting them in the two leftmost slots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "You also need to have at least one Flux in the anvil to aid the welding process. Then, while both items are hot enough to weld - the tooltip will say \"Can Weld\" - you must use any Hammer on the anvil. You will hear a hammering sound and the items will be welded together. They can then be extracted by using on the anvil with an empty hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Anvils are an important tool required for metalworking, as they allow you to work and weld metal ingots into various different forms. Anvils can be useful for both Working, which is used to form one piece of metal into another, or Welding, which is used to fuse two metal items into one solid piece.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",

          "text": "All types of metal anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anvils",

          "text": "Anvils can be crafted with Double Ingots of their respective metal. For your first anvil, you must weld double ingots first on a Stone Anvil.",
