          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "In the middle of the anvil screen, there is a bar with two colored indicators. The green pointer, is your current working progress. The red pointer, is the target. Your goal is to line up the current progress, with the target. In order to do this, you can use the green and red action buttons, which move your current progress a certain amount, depending on the action taken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Green actions will always move your target right, and Red actions will always move your progress left. Note that if you move your target off of the progress bar, you will have overworked your item - you will lose the ingot. However, while working, you must also match the rules...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "The rules, are the two or three icons shown on the top of the anvil interface. They represent specific actions that must be taken, at specific times, in order for your working to be a success. For example, a rule could be Bend Second Last, meaning the second to last action you take must be a Bend action.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Working Rules",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Your last three actions are shown right underneath the rules. When a rule is satisfied, its outline will change to green. Success occurs when all rules are satisfied. Finally, you have to be mindful of your item's temperature. Metals can only be worked when they are above a certain temperature where the tooltip shows \"Can Work\". You may take an item out and re-heat it during the working process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Working can be tedious, and take many steps to get correct. However, there is a reward for being efficient. Some items, such as tool heads, when they are worked in a low or minimal amount of steps, receive a Forging Bonus based on how efficiently they were forged. This bonus will then apply to tools that the item is used in, for example, a pickaxe head used to make a pickaxe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Forging Bonuses",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Perfectly Forged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.title",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "There are four tiers of forging bonus: - Poorly Forged - Well Forged - Expertly Forged - Perfectly Forged These bonuses increase the power of your tool - making it break less often, mine faster, and/or do more damage in combat, depending on the tool.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "Welding is a process through which two items are fused together to create a new item. Welding works the same whether on a Stone Anvil or a metal anvil. First, you must place the two items you want to weld on the anvil. You can do this either by using the items on the anvil, or by opening the anvil interface and inserting them in the two leftmost slots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/anvils.json",
          "text": "You also need to have at least one Flux in the anvil to aid the welding process. Then, while both items are hot enough to weld - the tooltip will say \"Can Weld\" - you must use any Hammer on the anvil. You will hear a hammering sound and the items will be welded together. They can then be extracted by using on the anvil with an empty hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfc:metal/anvil/copper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Slot: Back",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Copper Anvil",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfc:metal/anvil/copper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Cu",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Copper Anvil",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfc:metal/anvil/copper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 2016 mB of Copper (at Orange٭٭٭٭)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Copper Anvil",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "tfc:blast/off",
      "label": "Blast off!",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 1,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/blast_furnace",
          "confidence": 0.65
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Blast off!",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Blast off!",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Craft a blast furnace",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Blast off!",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A Blast Furnace is an advanced device which is used in the creation of Steel. By mixing Iron Ores, Coke or Anthacite, and Flux in a controlled, hot environment, you can create a stronger metal than cast or wrought iron. To obtain a blast furnace, you will first need a Crucible and a lot of Wrought Iron Plates.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Crafting a blast furnace itself requires a Crucible, along with some of the Double Wrought Iron Plates you will need.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "You will then need to construct the blast furnace, along with its Chimney. The chimney must be composed out of Reinforced Fire Bricks, as they are strong enough to withstand the intense heat. These can be crafted from fire brick blocks, and either cast iron or wrought iron plates. The plates can be applied to each exposed face of the bricks by + with the plate in hand, or crafted into Blast Furnace Insulation.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.name",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A blast furnace with a minimum height chimney.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "The blast furnace's chimney can be up to five layers - each layer requiring four Fire Bricks and twelve Wrought Iron Plates to complete. Having more layers increases the total capacity of the blast furnace, allowing it to smelt more steel at once. Each chimney layer, up to a maximum of five, allows the blast furnace to hold four additional ore items.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "In order to use the blast furnace, you must drop items in the top of the chimney - for steel production, you must add an equal number of items of Iron Ores and Flux. Any iron ores or items that are able to melt into Cast Iron will do. You will also need to add Coke or Anthacite, which will be consumed as the blast furnace works.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Using the blast furnace will open the blast furnace interface, seen to the right. In this interface, you will see meters for both ore and fuel contents of the blast furnace. The top right slot must have a Tuyere, which is a metal pipe used to funnel air into the blast furnace, required to reach the hottest temperatures to smelt steel. A tuyere can be smithed on an Anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace Interface",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "You will also need a Bellows in order for the Blast Furnace to reach a temperature which will melt iron. This can be placed on any of the four sides of the blast furnace.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A full size blast furnace with bellows and crucible attached.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Finally, to get started, light the blast furnace with a Fire Starter or a Flint and Steel. It will begin to heat the ores inside. Make sure that the blast furnace continues to have fuel, and use the bellows to add air to the blast furnace after its internal temperature has reached the maximum for charcoal. After the ores inside heat up, they will melt and convert into Pig Iron.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "This liquid metal will drip into any metal fluid container placed immediately below the blast furnace, such as a Crucible. It can be cast into ingot molds from the output slot of the crucible and worked into Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A Blast Furnace is an advanced device which is used in the creation of Steel. By mixing Iron Ores, Charcoal, and Flux in a controlled, hot environment, you can create a stronger metal than cast or wrought iron. To obtain a blast furnace, you will first need a Crucible and a lot of Wrought Iron Sheets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Crafting a blast furnace itself requires a Crucible, along with some of the Wrought Iron Sheets you will need.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "You will then need to construct the blast furnace, along with its Chimney. The chimney must be composed out of Fire Bricks, as they are strong enough to withstand the intense heat. It must then be lined with Wrought Iron Sheets, for extra reinforcement. Stronger metals such as Steel can also be used for the sheets, if desired.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },

          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Using the blast furnace will open the blast furnace interface, seen to the right. In this interface, you will see meters for both ore and fuel contents of the blast furnace. The top right slot must have a Tuyere, which is a metal pipe used to funnel air into the blast furnace, required to reach the hottest temperatures to smelt steel. A tuyere can be smithed on an Anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace Interface",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "You will also need a Bellows in order for the Blast Furnace to reach a temperature which will melt iron. This can be placed on any of the four sides of the blast furnace.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "A full size blast furnace with bellows and crucible attached.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "Finally, to get started, light the blast furnace with a Fire Starter or a Flint and Steel. It will begin to heat the ores inside. Make sure that the blast furnace continues to have fuel, and use the bellows to add air to the blast furnace after its internal temperature has reached the maximum for charcoal. After the ores inside heat up, they will melt and convert into Pig Iron.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/blast_furnace.json",
          "text": "This liquid metal will drip into any metal fluid container placed immediately below the blast furnace, such as a Crucible. It can be cast into ingot molds from the output slot of the crucible and worked into Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft Blast Furnace Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "So, you finally found that Kaolinite and are probably feeling pretty excited about making a 5-level Blast Furnace. Here are some tips before you go wild!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Before you use all your fire clay on 20 fire bricks, note that once you get into the steam age, you can craft it for significantly cheaper. This allows you to save Kaolinite and Graphite for Casting Tables.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "Preheating Metal",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "To convert the iron to steel, the blast furnace must heat the iron inside to Brilliant White. On the first operation, the metal warms up along with the blast furnace. However, subsequent operations will take a similar amount of time despite the furnace being at temp already, as it needs to heat the metal from cold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "If the metal is hot when added into the blast furnace, it will take less time to reach brilliant white. Consider heating your metal in a Charcoal Forge before putting it into an already-hot furnace to save time, especially with smaller blast furnaces that process less at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You'll need lots of Steel for the Steam and LV ages. Lots of steel. The Electric Blast Furnace is far off, so maybe think about optimizing your steel production.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You can pump liquid directly out of the blast furnace using a Create Mechanical Pump. Pump into a Mold Table and extract the ingots with a hopper or chute: Easy automation! You can automate the bellows with a Deployer too! Just be careful, as using the bellows excessively consumes more fuel and breaks your tuyere faster. You can process the resulting Pig Iron and High Carbon Steel in a Forge Hammer or Helve Hammer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips.json",
          "text": "You can automatically replace broken tuyeres with a hopper or item pipe facing into the blast furnace.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Blast Furnace Tips",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 7
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/bloomery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/bloomery.json",
          "text": "Ironworks",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Ironworks",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/bloomery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/bloomery.json",
          "text": "Craft a bloomery",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Ironworks",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a device used to smelt Iron Ore into Iron Blooms which can be worked into Wrought Iron. Some common iron ores are Hematite, Limonite, and Magnetite. These ores melt into Cast Iron rather than Wrought Iron. All iron-bearing items melt into Cast Iron. To turn them into usable iron, the bloomery is needed. Any iron item can be used in the bloomery, including iron tools and cast iron ingots!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The bloomery is made from 8 Bronze Double Plates.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "A Bloomery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.name",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "A minimum size bloomery. The bloomery block will open and close with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The bloomery can contain up to a maximum of 48 Inputs, with 16 items per layer of the chimney. To add layers to the chimney, stack up two more layers of stone blocks. To add items to the bloomery, climb up to the top and throw items inside. A tower of grey ore should form. You can use both Charcoal and Coal as fuel for your bloomery.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The bloomery consumes 1 item of Fuel and 144 mB of Cast Iron to produce one Bloom.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "If you don't want to do the math, we recommend 25 metal ore dusts and 22 fuel items. After filling the bloomery with a combination of Fuel and Ore, light the bloomery doors, and wait 15 hours for the bloomery to smelt. When the bloomery shuts off, it leaves behind a Bloom block. This contains Raw Iron Blooms which can be obtained by mining the Bloom repeatedly with a pickaxe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The Bloom Block",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.name",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "A large Bloom.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The Raw Iron Bloom must be worked in a anvil to make Refined Iron Bloom.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "The Refined Iron Bloom must be worked in a anvil to make Wrought Iron Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/bloomery.json",
          "text": "- If the bloomery finds itself with more items contained than it can handle based on its chimney, it will try to spit them out the front. - To retrieve your items from a bloomery that is not lit, do not break the molten block tower. Break the bloomery block. - Blooms will only melt into cast iron, not wrought iron. They must be worked!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Bloomery",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 10
        },

          "text": "Craft a quern and a handstone",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern.json",
          "text": "The Quern is a device for grinding items. It can crush raw ores for processing, as well as make powders, dyes, and some other items. It is assembled from a Base and Handstone.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Quern",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern.json",
          "text": "The base of the quern can be crafted with three smooth stone and three of any other Stone blocks. Stone blocks can be acquired via extraction, and smooth blocks with the chisel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Quern",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 11
        },

          "text": "The quern needs a Handstone to operate.",

          "label": "Quern",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 11
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern.json",

          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Quern",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 11
        },
        {

          "text": "Use on the top of the handstone to add items.",

          "label": "Quern",

          "recipe_ref_count": 5,

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern.json",
          "text": "Use the handle to spin the handstone.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Quern",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 11
        },
        {

        {

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/quern.json",
          "text": "Ores can be crushed to obtain more metal.",

          "recipe_ref_count": 5,

          "text": "The quern is used to make various Powders from dust, like Sulfur. Gems can also be ground into powder.",

          "count": 11
        },

          "text": "Crops can be processed with a knife or quern to acquire Grain, which can further be ground into Flour.",

    {
      "id": "firmalife:palm/stomping/barrel",
      "label": "Palm Stomping Barrel",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 1,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "firmalife:crafting/wood/palm_stomping_barrel",
          "confidence": 0.65
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "The Barrel is a device that can hold both items and fluids. The central slot is used to hold items. Fluids are shown in the tank on the left side, and can be added to the barrel by placing a filled bucket or jug in the top left slot. They can be removed by placing an empty fluid container in the same slot. Using on the block with a bucket also works.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "The barrel interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels can be Sealed. Sealing allows the barrel to be broken while keeping its contents stored. It also allows for the execution of some recipes. In the interface, sealing can be toggled with the grey button on the right side. Using on the barrel with an empty hand while holding also toggles the seal.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "- Barrels can be filled by clicking an empty one on a fluid block in the world. - Barrels will slowly fill up with water in the rain. - Icicles melting above barrels also adds water to them. - Sealing a barrel will eject items that are not in the center slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrel Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels are an important device used for mixing various fluids and items together. To use a Barrel recipe, the barrel must be filled with the correct ratio of fluid and items required. Depending on the recipe, it may need to be Sealed for a period of time to allow the contents to transform.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "If the contents of a barrel does not match the ratio required for the recipe, any excess fluid or items will be lost. If the recipe is one which converts instantly, however, you will always need at least enough items to fully convert the fluid, according to the recipe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Limewater is made by adding Flux to a barrel of Water. A single Flux is required for every 500 mB of Water. Limewater is a reagent used in Leather Making, and is used to make Mortar.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Tannin is an acidic fluid, made by dissolving bark from certain Logs in a barrel of Water. Oak, Birch, Chestnut, Douglas Fir, Hickory, Maple, and Sequoia are all able to be used to create Tannin.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "A couple barrel recipes operate by mixing two fluids at a certain ratio. This is done by taking a filled bucket of one of the ingredients, and putting it in the fluid addition slot of a barrel that has the required amount of the other fluid. This is done for making Milk Vinegar, where Vinegar is added to Milk at a 9:1 ratio. Vinegar is also added in the same ratio to Salt Water to make Brine.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels have the ability to cool hot items. Put a hot item in a barrel of Water, Olive Oil, or Salt Water, and it will quickly bring its temperature down.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels have the ability to Dye and Bleach items. Most color-able things, like carpet, candles, and Alabaster can be dyed by sealing them in a barrel of dye or bleached by sealing them in a barrel of lye. See the dye chapter for more information.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels can preserve items in Vinegar. Vinegar is made by sealing Fruit in a barrel of Alcohol. For information on how that process works, see the relevant page.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Barrels placed against the side of a block work the same as vertical barrels, but have restrictions on placement. For example, they must have a Barrel Rack added to them with in order to have another sideways barrel above them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Sideways Barrels",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.name",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Sideways barrels show their sealed state using the Tap. If the tap is sideways, it is sealed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "Unsealed sideways barrels automatically drain their fluid into open barrels (and other fluid containers) placed below the tap. The block the tap extends into must be a free air block in order for fluid to be able to drain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/barrels.json",
          "text": "A barrel dripping.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Barrels",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfc:wood/barrel/palm",
          "source": "runtime-items",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/categories/modes",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/categories/modes.json",
          "text": "Different building modes for the gadgets",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Ovens are a great way of cooking lots of food in a way that improves their shelf life. Oven-baked food decays at 90% of the rate of regular food. Ovens are a multiblock structure consisting of a Bottom Oven, Top Oven, and optionally Chimneys. These blocks start off as clay, and must be Cured by raising their temperature to a certain amount for long enough. Oven Appliances extend oven functionality.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the top oven.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the bottom oven.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the oven chimney .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Ovens are insulated with Bricks, other oven blocks, or anything that can insulate a Forge. This means you can use stone blocks, if you want!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The Peel is the only safe way to remove hot items from an Oven. Just on it while holding it to retrieve items. Otherwise, you may get burned!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The Oven first consists of the Top Oven placed on top of the Bottom Oven. All sides of each oven part, besides the front face, should then be covered with Oven Insulation blocks, as covered two pages ago. You may choose to use Oven Chimneys as insulation. Placing a stack of chimneys directly behind the oven causes the smoke from the oven to travel up and out of it. If you don't do this, smoke will quickly fill up your house, which is very distracting!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "An example oven structure, uncured and cured.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The Bottom Oven is used to hold fuel, which may only be logs. Press to add or remove them. The bottom oven is also the part of the oven which may be lit with a Firestarter or other tool. It transfers heat contained in it to the top oven.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "The Top Oven contains the items that are being cooked. It will draw heat from the Bottom Oven and slowly release it over time. This means that even if your fuel runs out, your Top Oven can continue to work for a little while. Adding items to it is as simple as pressing . Remember to use a Peel to remove the items after.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Curing Oven blocks is easy, but requires patience. Simply start running your Bottom Oven as you would normally, and then wait. If an oven block is above 600 degrees for about 80 seconds, it will cure itself and any oven blocks around it. The curing effect will pass all the way up chimneys nearby.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Crafting oven insulation for your Bottom Oven allows you to remove the need for insulating it on the sides and back. It does not remove the need for the chimney. Use to apply it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Countertops are aesthetic blocks that count as oven insulation, and have an appearance that matches that of oven blocks. They are a nice aesthetic choice for your kitchen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Ovens also have Finishes that can be used to change their appearance. These finishes are applied to the basic brick stage of the oven (or brick blocks themselves), and are cosmetic. Finishes can be mixed and matched. They are applied with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:bricks",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "(Na₂LiAl₂Si₂(H₂O)₆)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Bricks",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []

          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Curing Oven blocks is easy, but requires patience. Simply start running your Bottom Oven as you would normally, and then wait. If an oven block is above 600 degrees for about 80 seconds, it will cure itself and any oven blocks around it. The curing effect will pass all the way up chimneys nearby.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Crafting oven insulation for your Bottom Oven allows you to remove the need for insulating it on the sides and back. It does not remove the need for the chimney. Use to apply it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Countertops are aesthetic blocks that count as oven insulation, and have an appearance that matches that of oven blocks. They are a nice aesthetic choice for your kitchen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/ovens.json",
          "text": "Ovens also have Finishes that can be used to change their appearance. These finishes are applied to the basic brick stage of the oven (or brick blocks themselves), and are cosmetic. Finishes can be mixed and matched. They are applied with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Ovens",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 16
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Way off on the far reaches of Mars, you can find both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {

          "text": "The Climate Station is a block that manages the Greenhouse and the Cellar. When its corresponding multiblock is built correctly, it will show water on its sides. When it is invalid, it will show ice. The Climate Station must be placed on the first level of the multiblock, touching a wall. If it is not touching a wall, you may it with a block that is part of the multiblock you are trying to make to tell it what to look for.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Climate Station",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 5
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/climate_station",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/climate_station.json",
          "text": "The climate station in its valid and invalid state.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Climate Station",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/climate_station",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/climate_station.json",
          "text": "- It updates periodically on its own, or when placed/broken. - When a climate station updates, it tells all the blocks inside the multiblock that they can operate. For example, it lets Cheese begin aging. - Press to force update the Climate Station and the blocks inside the multiblock.",

          "recipe_ref_count": 1,

          "text": "Climate Station Tips",

          "text": "The climate station is crafted like this.",

          "text": "A note about greenhouse construction: The largest greenhouse is 31x31x31, centered on the climate station, or 15 blocks in each direction.     {
      "id": "ad_astra:cracked/moon/stone/bricks/from/smelting/moon/stone/bricks",

      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "advancement",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
